1jehuang/jcode ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.99.1 Stable2026-10-22T14:56:09Z

OpenAI request fix and stability

Highlights

  • Fix OpenAI models failing every turn with invalid_request_error due to a rejected tool schema
  • Recover the TUI from a vanished or unresponsive server instead of retrying forever
  • Native Muse subscription login and Venice AI provider support

Improvements

  • Add configurable Kagi web search backend
  • Add persistent /latex rendering controls
  • Show live stopwatch, context, and token stats per swarm worker

Fixes

  • Honor the configured model for fresh sessions
  • Report token counts for OpenAI native compaction
  • Reject stale patch hunks before any file changes
  • Keep swarm workers alive across reloads
  • Fix OAuth credential handoff and stale daemon token recovery
  • Fix Kitty images in Zellij

Full changelog: https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/compare/v0.99.0...v0.99.1

Platform availability

  • Linux x86_64: available
  • Linux aarch64: available
  • macOS Apple Silicon: available
  • macOS Intel: available
  • Windows x86_64: available
  • Windows ARM64: available
  • FreeBSD x86_64: available

Windows executables are unsigned. Azure Artifact Signing is not configured and the repository's existing WINDOWS_SIGNING_REQUIRED=false override allowed unsigned publication. Windows may show publisher or SmartScreen warnings. <!-- jcode-platform-availability:end -->

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.21.30 Stable2026-10-22T13:05:58Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.30

0.21.30 is a patch release for Team monitor-path dispatch serialization (4 commits, 10 files, +324/−78; PRs #3879, #3880, #3882).

Highlights

  • Team monitor-path dispatch serialization (#3879, #3880, #3882): The v0.21.29 release fixed dispatch retry consistency, mailbox wake acknowledgment, and damaged mailbox recovery for the primary dispatch path. This release extends those same fixes to the secondary monitor-path delivery route (monitorTeam → deliverPendingMailboxMessages → dispatchPendingMailboxMessage):
    • Monitor-path wake serialization (#3879): Monitor-created wake conditions now participate in the same serialization barrier applied to acknowledgments, ensuring that wake acknowledgment only occurs after all messages from that wake are complete.
    • Monitor-path acknowledgement race fix (#3880): Resolves a race condition in the monitor path where mailbox acknowledgements can race with dispatch, leaving messages undelivered. The fix ensures dispatch requests are enqueued inside the serialization barrier with final re-validation.
    • Stale notification skipping and proof (#3882): Implements stale monitor notification detection and skipping, with comprehensive test coverage proving the race condition is resolved across both primary and monitor paths.

Compatibility

  • No breaking API changes; all fixes are internal serialization improvements.
  • Existing sessions and Team dispatch workflows remain unaffected; recovery is transparent.
  • Monitor-based event delivery is now consistent with primary dispatch retry paths.

Validation

  • Pinned candidate 0ec816c21af5cacfcd2f013124fce1b11127c39a passed latest-per-name dev CI: 17 checks succeeded and 8 conditional checks were skipped, with no failures (verified at 12:17Z on 2026-10-22).
  • Release branch CI passed with all required checks green.
  • All code review findings addressed.
  • Local gates pass: build, typecheck, and lint.

Merged PR inventory

PR Author Title
#3879 @shenyankm fix(team): serialize monitor-created wakes with acknowledgements

v0.21.29 Stable2026-10-22T07:58:18Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.29

0.21.29 is a patch release for Team dispatch state recovery and Codex startup UI protection (1 commit, 16 files, +616/−68; PR #3876).

Highlights

  • Team dispatch state recovery and startup UI protection (#3876): This release fixes three related regressions that can occur under dispatch retry and multiple-message scenarios:
    • Dispatch retry correctness: A runtime dispatch event can commit before its JSON projection is published. Retrying against an empty, missing, or stale projection can duplicate a request, coalesce into an already delivered request, or leave requests behind during worker retirement. The fix ensures dispatch retries use consistent, durable projections.
    • Mailbox wake handling: Multiple mailbox messages can share one wake condition. Acknowledging the wake's original message before other messages are delivered can leave the wake active after the mailbox is fully delivered, preventing further message processing. The fix ensures wake acknowledgment only after all messages from that wake are complete.
    • Damaged mailbox recovery: A damaged or unreadable per-worker mailbox was previously treated as empty, allowing a later send to recreate it with inconsistent state. The fix treats damaged mailboxes as undeliverable, preventing silent message loss and worker proliferation.
    • Codex startup UI safety: Inbox/notification text can be injected into Codex startup or folder-trust screens, disrupting the initial prompt. The fix prevents notification payload from being rendered during Codex startup and folder-trust UI phases.
    • Readiness record accuracy: The Codex startup screen and folder-trust screen are no longer misidentified as a ready prompt, improving diagnostics and state tracking.

Compatibility

  • No breaking API changes; all fixes are internal recovery and UI isolation improvements.
  • Existing sessions and dispatch workflows remain unaffected; recovery is transparent.
  • Codex startup and folder-trust workflows are now more robust against concurrent notifications.

Validation

  • Pinned candidate 71029cdfc80946da1e0b6770d47e31aae38cf5ab passed latest-per-name dev CI: 17 checks succeeded and 8 conditional checks were skipped, with no failures (run #39737386059, concluded 07:19Z).
  • Release branch CI passed with all required checks green.
  • All code review findings addressed.
  • Known gap: Codex review not completed due to usage limit constraints, but validated through code review and green CI gate.

2026.10.6

Highlights

  • Patched Node runtime required: OpenClaw now requires Node 24.22.1+ on the 24 line or 26.12.1+ on the 26 line, which reject TLS chains signed with the compromised certificate keys addressed in the October 2026 Node.js security releases. Docker images ship Node 24.22.1, installers select the patched runtimes, and openclaw update refuses with recovery guidance before installing on an older Node instead of breaking the running install. Source builds now pin pnpm 12.15.0. (#179992) Thanks @joshavant.
  • Safer updates and recovery: package swaps now refuse unsafe service states, Windows and macOS package-manager updates recover from platform-specific failures, and frozen Gateway startup owners can be retired cleanly. (#171101, #170940, #171936, #171678) Thanks @RomneyDa, @homeworld520, @Yolo521-bot, @stop-time, @AntaresGG, and @TommyLei666.
  • Data-preserving migrations and backups: Doctor and migration paths now refuse malformed or lossy workspace imports, preserve malformed Notes bytes, reject unsafe non-UTF-8 wiki rewrites, keep deeply nested SQLite backups, and retain staging evidence when restore inspection fails. (#172293, #172953, #172742, #174573, #168583, #170867, #172028) Thanks @wangmiao0668000666, @RomneyDa, @Leon-SK668, @ericcurtin, @Yolo521-bot, @obviyus, and @altaywtf.
  • Reliable delivery across restarts: queued turns, watched child results, dead-lettered deliveries, and streamed replies now survive recovery without disappearing, duplicating, or producing false missing-answer notices. (#170384, #169489, #169932, #173798) Thanks @RomneyDa, @huyengiang101086-spec, and @Yolo521-bot.
  • Provider and model resilience: GPT-5.6 tool turns, OpenRouter DeepSeek tool calls, LM Studio overflow recovery, parallel OpenAI reasoning settings, and model retry/fallback budgets now behave consistently. (#176709, #175058, #169189, #169322, #164808) Thanks @RomneyDa, @Linux2010, @ZhimingQ, @xpeng5278-web, and @anyech.
  • Windows and mobile stability: fresh Windows state stores, Proxy-backed environments, and stored Anthropic credentials no longer block startup, while iOS Talk and chat connectivity recover from first-reply and timeout failures. (#176174, #176194, #174571, #168416, #169912) Thanks @RomneyDa, @tjarrau-dev, @skyzhao1223, @easyteacher, @scalesmart, @masatohoshino, @Takhoffman, and @Solvely-Colin.
  • Agent and tool-call recovery: unfinished tools now time out visibly, context overflow compacts after steered tool results, malformed calls continue after earlier refusals, large patch hunks avoid argument-limit crashes, and stopped yielded tasks stay stopped.   • Scheduled follow-ups: same-conversation follow-ups scheduled from an authorized operator turn keep that operator's current authority when they run, and one-shot continuations can call next_check to check back later. (#176422)
  • Media and TTS: time out slow hosted speech synthesis and report playback cache, probe, and quota failures rather than silently dropping cleanup errors. (#175614, #175634, #174668) Thanks @RomneyDa.

nexu-io/open-design ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
open-design-v0.26.1Open Design 0.26.1 — Intelligent Router Stable2026-10-22T12:52:42Z

English

Meet Skill Discovery: describe what you want to create, and Open Design finds the relevant Skills to guide the work—from your first idea to the next revision.

Your idea should be the starting point. In 0.26.1, Skill Discovery helps Open Design understand the kind of work you want to make and choose the Skills that fit. You can focus on the result, with less manual setup before creating and more continuity as you refine it. Alongside this, smoother project starts, connected feedback, and steadier previews keep your work moving.

📥 Download / 下载

Platform / 平台 Installer / 安装包
macOS Apple silicon Download · SHA-256
macOS Intel Download · SHA-256
Windows x64 Download · SHA-256

🧠 Skill Discovery: the right Skills for what you have in mind

Describe the result you want. Skill Discovery selects the deliverable type and reads its native Skill guidance, giving Open Design a task-specific starting point. It supports eleven deliverable types, including interactive prototypes and motion work. (#8804)

  • Start with your intent. Your explicit request takes priority when it conflicts with a selected task type. Skills and templates you mention provide compatible guidance and references while keeping the requested deliverable in focus.
  • Bring the right guidance into the work. Open Design reads the relevant Skill instructions for the chosen type, so you spend less time finding and selecting them yourself.
  • Keep the direction as you iterate. Follow-up work carries the chosen Skill guidance forward, keeping revisions consistent. If you ask only for a plan or clarification, that request does not silently become permission to produce the final work.

🔥 Highlights

  • 🔗 Share the work, not a pile of screenshots. Publish a design file as one public link, stop it when the review is over, and resume the same link later. Comments left on the shared page flow back to the file, with unread feedback waiting where you work. (#8408, #8859, #8867, #8904) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • 🤖 Bring your own connection — and keep the Agent in the loop. Open Design can use available registered runtimes, and the same BYOK connection works from the app or the od CLI. Your project preview stays isolated to its own work, even when two projects use the same browser storage. (#8702) Thanks @itscheems.
  • 🚪 Home now opens straight into the real project. Send an idea from Home and the project is already there: one steady workspace, one Working state, and no disappearing first message while creation or uploads catch up. (#8267) Thanks @Siri-Ray.
  • 🎬 Motion keeps its rhythm. Video-motion previews stop jumping through music when the browser hiccups; while a video is still being made, you can watch the picture without unexpected sound, then hear the finished result normally. (#8777) Thanks to the Open Design crew.

✨ Added

🎨 Studio, prototypes and media

  • Use your organisation's image sources without a key hunt. Open Design can use configured company stock sources while retaining source information and keeping credits out of the finished page unless you ask for them. (#8691)
  • Chinese community help now leads to Feishu. Simplified and Traditional Chinese entry points point to the official Feishu community, while other locales continue to use Discord. (#8884) Thanks @joeylee12629-star.

🧠 Agents, workspaces and CLI

  • Your MCP tools follow the workspace you selected. Switch workspaces, reconnect, and the bridge now sees the projects and artifacts you actually have open. (#7619) Thanks @roian6.

⚡ Performance

  • Spend less time waiting for a Motion export. Desktop video capture is faster while preserving the requested frame size, frame rate, and image quality. (#8798) Thanks @PerishCode.

🐛 Fixed

🧩 Home and projects

  • Task choices and the More menu feel like Home again. The task-type controls, cancellation behaviour, compact-window layout, and keyboard navigation are back where your draft can rely on them. (#8947)
  • Artifact actions stay visible in chat. Project conversations keep the familiar action styling for generated artifacts, and Home no longer surfaces the obsolete plugin hover card. (#8978, #8911) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • Finished work stays finished. A brief history lookup failure no longer turns an existing delivered run into a failure when you reopen the project. (#8804)

🎞️ Preview and sharing

  • Keep commenting while the preview changes. A saved artifact can refresh with a comment draft open; a missing target asks for reselection instead of throwing the draft away. (#8904) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • An existing shared file keeps its identity. If local share bookkeeping is missing, Open Design recovers a verified cloud file and its original link rather than silently minting a replacement. (#8859, #8867) Thanks @alchemistklk.

🙏 Thanks to everyone who shipped 0.26.1

@alchemistklk · @itscheems · @roian6 · @Siri-Ray · @PerishCode · @joeylee12629-star

中文

全新 Skill Discovery，从你的创作意图出发，发现并选用合适的 Skill。少一些手动配置，从第一个想法到下一轮修改，让创作更贴近你想要的结果。

说出你想做什么，让 Open Design 更懂得如何开始。0.26.1 带来 **Skill Discovery：理解你想创作的作品类型，选择对应 Skill，并在后续修改中延续创作方向。**从开始创作前的能力选择，到作品生成后的继续打磨，你可以把更多注意力留给想法本身。更顺畅的项目启动、分享反馈和预览体验，也让整个创作过程更加连贯。

📥 下载正式版安装包

🧠 Skill Discovery：更懂你的想法，也更懂该用什么能力

描述你想要的结果，Skill Discovery 会识别作品类型，选择并读取对应的原生 Skill 指引，让 Open Design 带着适合这类任务的方法开始创作。目前覆盖 11 类交付物，包括交互原型、动效等创作场景。 (#8804)

  • 从你的意图出发。 当明确需求与手动选择的任务类型不一致时，优先尊重你说出的需求；你提到的 Skill 和模板会作为相容的指导与参考，让创作围绕你真正想要的作品展开。
  • 让合适的能力参与创作。 根据作品类型读取对应 Skill 的具体指引，减少你在开始前查找、选择技能的操作。
  • 继续修改，延续创作方向。 继续修改时，沿用已选 Skill 的创作指导，保持前后连贯。你只想讨论方案或澄清需求时，也会尊重这个阶段的意图，不擅自开始制作。

🔥 重点更新

  • 🔗 分享作品，而不是一堆截图。 把设计文件发布为一个公开链接；评审结束时停止分享，需要继续时仍沿用同一个链接。公开页面上的评论会同步回文件，未读反馈就在你工作的地方等你处理。 (#8408、#8859、#8867、#8904) 感谢 @alchemistklk。
  • 🤖 带上自己的连接，把 Agent 留在流程里。 Open Design 可以使用已注册且可用的运行时；同一套 BYOK 连接既能在应用中使用，也能通过 od CLI 使用。即使两个项目用了相同的浏览器存储键，预览状态也各自独立。 (#8702) 感谢 @itscheems。
  • 🚪 从 Home 发送，直接进入真实项目。 从 Home 发出想法后，项目工作区立即就位：只有一个稳定的界面、一个 Working 状态；创建或上传较慢时，第一条消息也不会消失。 (#8267) 感谢 @Siri-Ray。
  • 🎬 动效预览终于跟得上节奏。 浏览器短暂卡顿时，视频动效不会反复跳音；生成期间可以看画面而不会意外出声，完成后仍可正常听到成片声音。 (#8777) 感谢 Open Design 团队。

✨ 新增

🎨 工作台、原型与素材

  • 使用团队素材库，不必再找 Key。 Open Design 可以使用已配置的公司素材来源，保留来源信息；除非你明确要求，成品页面不会出现署名。 (#8691)
  • 中文社区入口现在前往飞书。 简体中文和繁体中文的入口会打开官方飞书社区，其他语言继续使用 Discord。 (#8884) 感谢 @joeylee12629-star。

🧠 Agent、工作区与 CLI

  • MCP 会跟随你正在使用的工作区。 切换工作区并重新连接后，工具看到的是当前项目和产物，不再误落到其他工作区。 (#7619) 感谢 @roian6。

⚡ 性能

  • 少等一会儿动效导出。 桌面端视频抓帧更快，同时保持请求的分辨率、帧率和画面质量。 (#8798) 感谢 @PerishCode。

🐛 修复

🧩 Home 与项目

  • 任务选择和“更多”菜单重新可靠。 任务类型控件、取消选择、短窗口布局和键盘导航都回到能放心保留草稿的位置。 (#8947)
  • 对话中的作品操作重新清晰可见。 生成作品的操作样式恢复熟悉的样子，Home 也不再显示过时的插件悬浮卡。 (#8978、#8911) 感谢 @alchemistklk。
  • 已完成的工作仍然是已完成。 重新打开项目时，即使历史状态短暂无法读取，已有交付物也不会被错误显示为失败。 (#8804)

🎞️ 预览与分享

  • 预览变化时，评论可以继续写。 保存作品后，即使评论草稿还开着，预览也会刷新；评论目标消失时会提示重新选择，而不是丢掉草稿。 (#8904) 感谢 @alchemistklk。
  • 已有的分享文件保留自己的身份。 本地分享记录缺失时，Open Design 会恢复已验证的云端文件与原链接，而不会悄悄新建替代链接。 (#8859、#8867) 感谢 @alchemistklk。

🙏 感谢参与 0.26.1 的贡献者

@alchemistklk · @itscheems · @roian6 · @Siri-Ray · @PerishCode · @joeylee12629-star

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.41 Stable2026-10-22T12:40:28Z

The engine moves to senpi 2026.10.21-1: engine hosts start faster and idle far more quietly, Claude subscriptions use the native 1M context again, and desktop threads are lighter (#10740). A new engine host starts about 0.1 s faster, and an idle engine host wakes 405 times per 90 s instead of 1,106. Current Claude subscription models (Fable 5, Sonnet 5, Haiku 5.5, Opus 4.7 and later) get their native 1M window back without asking for extra usage, and the Claude subscription lane runs Claude Code 2.1.306. Each desktop thread's engine supervisor starts from its own small program instead of the whole CLI, and messages and tool cards no longer keep duplicate copies (about 640 bytes per message and 2 KB per tool card). Models that models.dev marks deprecated stay available, labeled deprecated, instead of disappearing (GitHub Copilot Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.4, Baseten Kimi K2 and others), and the model catalog is refreshed; some models get updated thinking levels (for example Nemotron 3 Ultra: off is now none). A message that was already delivered no longer stays listed under Steering, and with several accounts for one provider a usage limit stops failing turns once its reset has passed; a quota failure now says when it resets.

Memory sync refuses signatures under weak Ed25519 keys. Bun's WebCrypto accepts a signature made under a small-order public key, such as the identity key, for any message. The sync client now checks every key and signature it verifies itself, and refuses any keyring record that would bring a weak or malformed signing key into the account's keyring, including keys that appear only inside a fork-resolution's evidence. (#10743, fixes #10742)

Outside full access, computer use asks before it acts on your screen as a whole. Clicks, typing, key presses, scrolling, drags, pointer moves and holds aimed at the desktop or a display land on your real pointer and frontmost app, so they now need the same "Allow foreground computer control?" approval as foreground input; without it they fail with ControlRequired and nothing is sent. Input to one window in the background still needs no approval, and full access keeps working without a prompt, headless runs included. The macOS background check no longer moves your pointer back after you moved it, and a stopped session stays stopped when the desktop engine restarts. (#10788, #10790)

OmO counts how often you had to repeat a standing instruction (#10780, #10786). Rules like "never filter the monitor output" now live in system/agreements.md with a count. When you correct the agent on something an agreement already covers, the count goes up; after the third time (memory.agreements.needs_human_threshold) the agent is told the rule keeps being broken and you see a notice. /agreements lists them and lets you add your own, acknowledge one, or drop one; the agreements you add can only be changed by you. The design is inspired by dinobot by roach (PolyForm Noncommercial 1.0.0); no dinobot code was used.

The launcher deletes a legacy ~/.omo/credentials.json left by an intermediate sign-in build. The file held the device's private keys and account refresh material in plaintext, sometimes world-readable; upgrading removes it. Only the exact legacy shape is removed. (#10697, #10698)

Report a problem from inside OmO (#10546). /feedback (or /bug) sends a bug report or an idea to the OmO team in one step: your description plus your account, this device, versions, this conversation's recent messages, recent logs and your settings. Passwords, keys and tokens are removed first with the same redactor /share uses, your memory is never sent, and OMO_DISABLE_FEEDBACK=1 saves the report as a local file instead of uploading it. The desktop app's Report a problem dialog uses the same report.

A DAG node whose child dies on infrastructure is retried instead of taking its whole branch down. When a mass-ulw node's child stops answering its heartbeats or loses its host session, the node is now retried automatically, at most twice, with a backoff. Its dependents are skipped only if the retries fail too. Before, one starved wave failed and every later node was skipped (an overloaded machine skipped 54 of 61 nodes). Task failures and cancelled nodes behave as before, and the TUI and the desktop app share this behaviour (#10605, #10616).

Stopping a language server on Windows no longer raises a stray broken-pipe error. When an LSP server's pipe closed while OmO was shutting the server down, Windows could report the failed write after cleanup had already let go of it, and the EPIPE escaped as an unhandled error. Every pipe of a spawned language server now keeps an error handler for its whole life, so the failure is logged as ignored cleanup noise (#9601, #10725).

The frontend skill now covers HTML reports, figures and slides, and its stripe check catches square callouts. The skill now loads for any HTML, CSS or SVG made for visual output (a report printed to PDF, a figure, a diagram, a slide, an image for Notion or Slack), not only web UI. Its check-accent-stripes.ts linter fails a coloured bar along one side of a box whether the box is rounded or square, and a 2px-or-wider accent outline on a rounded card. Neutral hairlines, table rules, a blockquote's neutral rule, focus rings and input validation states stay allowed. The visualize skill runs the linter on every page before previewing or showing it (#10774, #10776).

ulw-research shows its report inline and checks a visual reproduction against the source (#10812). When the host can show an HTML page in the thread (show_html_page, html_render), the run ends with the report there, before the file paths, on every tier and even when the research was shortened; a built app or 3D scene shown inline no longer stands in for it. When the ask is to recreate something visual, such as a clip rebuilt in 3D, the run puts real frames of the source next to its own output at the same moments, writes down what differs, fixes the biggest gaps, and shows the side-by-side pairs in the report.

Changed

  • Combine macOS root-test shards and Codex/Senpi compatibility into one runner job, preserving their existing check names through Ubuntu status jobs. Full-matrix CI now requests two macOS runners per head instead of five, including unchanged native binary parity; fast paths skip the combined allocation (#10804).

  • CI: pull requests run the macOS and Windows legs only when a platform-specific path changes: any path a macOS/Windows-only CI step names (derived from the workflow), native crates, the launcher and platform packaging, the Windows Git Bash runtime, the Codex installer, platform-named sources, and the CI workflows. Pushes to dev and merge groups still run every OS, and the run-full-ci label forces the full matrix on a pull request (#10856). (#10859)

EpicGames/lore ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.11.0 Stable2026-10-22T12:37:53Z

The latest release of Lore, Epic Games' next-generation open source version control system.

Quickstart | Design | Documentation | Discord

Quick Install

Windows

irm https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EpicGames/lore/main/scripts/install.ps1 | iex

macOS / Linux

curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EpicGames/lore/main/scripts/install.sh | bash

Release Notes

Changes since v0.10.1.

Breaking changes

  • C API: the new LORE_EVENT_FILE_AUDIT event adds a member to lore_event_t; rebuild against the new lore.h
  • [environment.endpoint] user_url now names a lore.user.v1.UserService (UserGet, UserFind) instead of the UrcAuthApi GetUserInfo / GetUserId calls. With user_url set, the server also lists repositories there by default ([server.auth] repository_catalog = "user_service") and registers the repositories it creates and deletes there instead of calling RebacApi. Without it, clients ask auth_url as before
  • A client and the Lore service it relays to must come from the same build (message protocol version 2); restart the service after upgrading
  • Data is verified by the trust of its source (ADR-00023): local store reads no longer verify hashes, and data fetched from a remote is verified on receipt unless the remote is trusted (--trust-remote). C API: skip_verify on lore_storage_open_args_t is now an alias of trust_remote and skips verifying fetched data only
  • C API: lore_branch_merge_start_args_t gains link_paths and link_messages, changing its size; rebuild against the new lore.h. A zero-initialized struct keeps today's behavior
  • lore-server: refuses to start if [server.auth] resource_id_template or [server.auth.oidc] resource_template / scope_template lacks {id}, since such a template let one grant match every partition

Features

  • lore file audit <path> shows, for each line of a file, the revision that last changed it, grouped into ranges of consecutive lines (C API: lore_file_audit / lore_file_audit_async)
  • --trust-remote (C API: trust_remote on lore_global_args_t) skips hash verification of fetched data, keeping only a size check, for machines inside the remote's own infrastructure such as build servers. C callers must zero the whole struct, padding included, or stray bytes turn it on
  • C API: fragments on lore_storage_get_resolved_item_t delivers content piece by piece as stored (LORE_EVENT_STORAGE_GET_FRAGMENT), leaving decoding and verification to the reader, which can keep payloads compressed
  • lore auth login against an OIDC issuer uses the OAuth 2.0 device authorization grant (RFC 8628), opening the verification URL in the browser or printing it with --no-browser; --token-type <TYPE> --token <TOKEN> logs in with an existing token through RFC 8693 token exchange. Partition and repository operations send the access token, or a per-partition token when the server advertises resource_template / scope_template. ID tokens are never stored or sent; refresh and client credentials grants aren't implemented yet
  • The user identity is read from a token by the same rule on client and server: the identity claim (or https://lore.org/claims/identity), else sub. Tokens with an empty or non-string identity are refused, and [server.auth] identity_claim is removed
  • lore branch merge and lore branch merge start take --link-message <PATH> <MESSAGE>, as lore commit does
  • lore clone --offline / --cache prepares a clone so staging and committing work without a connection
  • lore service status reports the status of the running Lore service
  • LORE_STORE_RETAIN=1 keeps a process's stores loaded between repository calls, without holding their locks, for store_keep_alive_seconds (default 10). While it's set, don't share a store directory with processes of an earlier build
  • lore-server: [server.http] health_only = true serves only /health_check
  • lore-server metrics: gRPC requests record rpc.server.handler.duration, excluding request upload and pre-body work such as authorization, and BranchPush records its fragment verification load under lore.branch_push
  • contrib/bazel/: a Bazel remote execution server (Remote Execution API v2) keeping its action cache and content-addressable storage in Lore partitions

Fixes & Improvements

  • lore sync --reset replaces a directory with a file at the same path instead of failing with "Is a directory", and syncs, branch switches, resets, merges and view changes report each deleted file and directory via LORE_EVENT_REVISION_SYNC_FILE. Files and directories named .urc or .lore, in any case, are ignored by every command
  • lore commit and lore clone report the error that stopped them instead of Recursion task failed, and a failed clone no longer prints Clone complete (C API: LORE_EVENT_REPOSITORY_CLONE_END is sent only on success)
  • lore status: --scan of many paths runs in parallel, files are listed in stable path order, and modified files are no longer missed after a scan that dropped a reverted add or after a same-size re-save
  • Merges of divergent history (such as a sync with new local and remote revisions): lore file history attributes remote changes correctly, remote additions and earlier remote changes are kept, committing no longer fails in some cases, and the branch is reported ahead of the remote instead of diverged afterwards
  • Layers: committing a staged layer works when the parent tree holds only unstaged dirty changes, and several commands no longer copy the current revision's metadata, such as its reviewer, onto the next revision
  • Merge conflicts: a branch's change to a file is no longer dropped without a conflict in some add-after-delete cases, and resolving to one side no longer uses memory in proportion to the size of the tree
  • Links: committing through a link pinned at a merge revision no longer records a spurious merge, staging inside a nested link more than once no longer pushes intermediate states, and lore commit --link-message accepts any spelling of the link path
  • lore dirty inside a link, or on its mount, marks the change in the linked repository, so lore status and staging pick it up without a scan
  • Lore service: commands run with the caller's configuration and credentials, lore_storage_put / lore_storage_get (and their _resolved variants) work through the service, unreadable shared stores or instances are skipped at startup, and on Linux and macOS two services started at once no longer both run
  • Auth: the CLI sends tokens only to hosts named in their root_domains or aud claims, no longer to the iss host except for token exchange. With [server.auth] resource_claim set, repository lookups by name or id answer any verified caller, so clone, lore link add, lore layer add, repository info and repository delete no longer fail as not found before the caller holds a grant
  • Server write checks: pushing to a protected branch requires the push-protected action and the is_service_account claim no longer bypasses protection, so grant push-protected to mirroring accounts first. Generic MutableStore / MutableCompareAndSwap requests can no longer write repository metadata, branch metadata or branch latest pointers; use RepositoryMetadataSet, BranchMetadataSet and BranchPush
  • RevisionList: listing from a revision above the branch's latest no longer crashes the server, and paging through older history on a deep branch no longer times out with Cancelled
  • Pushing and committing: some large files no longer fail to commit or push, and a retried push or a gRPC push copying the same content to several files no longer fails with a missing fragment
  • Verification: caches, prefetches and immutable store queries verify fetched data unless the remote is trusted, fetching again once after asking the server to repair it, and corrupt local data is fetched again and replaced instead of crashing an unverified read
  • Reads: streaming reads and reads into files enforce the content size limit, a corrupt fragment list with out-of-order offsets fails the read instead of returning wrong data, and reads of fragmented content do less redundant work
  • Fetching: concurrent reads of the same missing content download it once, and caching runs at most 512 fetches at once per remote connection (LORE_CACHE_GETS), so reads a command needs right away don't queue behind it
  • lore-server with a remote store verifies data fetched from the backend and asks it to repair failures, refuses data in a codec it's built without (such as Oodle), size-checks data from replicas and peers, and no longer crashes on a truncated replica response
  • Oodle: the local store's Oodle migration finishes sooner, and a build without Oodle replaces such data with the remote's re-encoded copy, or reports NotSupported where there's no remote copy
  • Commands start faster, as opening a repository lists each store's index once; lore repository create syncs to disk only with --sync-data
  • Fix a deadlock between processes using one repository, such as the Lore service and a storage handle; stores are now locked only while in use, so idle handles no longer block other processes
  • Local store durability: transient I/O errors, failed or interrupted flushes, obliterates and index reorganizations no longer lose data, bring back removed entries or serve another file's data, and background index flushes now follow every change
  • A local store whose version file can't be read fails to open instead of re-migrating on every open; an affected store recovers at its next command connected to the remote
  • Obliterate in local stores is saved to disk before the data is zeroed and waits for reads in progress, and obliterated content no longer comes back when stored again under another context or re-encoded
  • Compaction does far less disk work and no longer stalls server stores for minutes; client stores never evict local-only data, and lore repository gc no longer swaps max_size and max_capacity
  • Storage sessions: handles reuse one server session per partition, so long-running processes are no longer refused every later call, and an unauthorized session is retried once with fresh authorization
  • Reconnects: storage handles and servers' remote stores reconnect after a failed connection, with a backoff of up to 30 seconds, QUIC streams that stop carrying requests reconnect instead of hanging, and reads and uploads use all the connections --max-connections allows
  • QUIC: the vendored quinn-proto moves to 0.11.19, fixing CVE-2026-25800 (unauthenticated remote memory exhaustion) and other upstream memory-exhaustion, stream-limit, Retry-token and path MTU issues
  • QUIC throughput: congestion control and stream ordering fixes, and the client asks for a larger UDP receive buffer; on Linux, set net.core.rmem_max to 524288 or more to allow it
  • C API: a call reading into data_out that stops part way no longer writes the caller's buffer after completion. data_out must stay valid until completion, and lore_shutdown is safe only once every call has completed

Release Assets

  • loreserver - Lore Server

hugohe3/ppt-master ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v6.7.3 Stable2026-10-22T11:43:24Z

Patch release: Edit Native and native-object fixes that stop a round trip from failing or silently replacing content, plus a readable fallback for native formulas outside PowerPoint.

Edit Native PPTX

  • An explicit Morph pair whose endpoint sits on an otherwise unchanged page no longer crashes with KeyError. The page keeps its source bytes, and only the shape name the pair needs is patched (604ecdec).
  • Moving an imported object that kept its source text body (for example Office Math that could not be rebuilt as a formula) restores that text body at the new position. Before, any move rebuilt the formulas as plain text. Edits that do change the text still rebuild it, and now warn when native math would become plain text (2e1658cc).
  • Imported charts and block formulas rebuilt during a round trip keep their source shape id. Before, export stopped with "Edited slide removed source animation target(s)" (0db9d903, cd445683).

Native charts and formulas

  • A new or rebuilt chart no longer takes the same part name as a chart restored from the source package. Before, the later write silently replaced the earlier chart's data. Exports without such a collision are byte-identical (970d9cc9).
  • Native formulas now include a fallback built from their SVG preview, so clients without Office Math support (LibreOffice, WPS) show the formula instead of nothing. PowerPoint keeps using the native math (dc7eab3f).
  • PPTX import rebuilds PPT Master formulas whose color is a theme color or translucent, instead of turning them into plain text boxes (029201fe).

Live preview editor

  • The preview canvas no longer stays blank with "SVG may be corrupted or missing the root <svg> element", and Save no longer writes ns0:-prefixed SVGs. This regression was only on main after v6.7.2. Thanks @ganeshmshetty (#323, #324).

Image to PPTX

  • The profile now defines how page-pixel geometry maps to the SVG canvas, so native text and shapes stay aligned with the base layers when the canvas differs from the source size (f2a20a0f, discussion #272).

Installation and docs

  • pip install -r requirements.txt no longer fails with UnicodeDecodeError on Chinese Windows (GBK locale) (69634d65, discussion #321).
  • README and the positioning docs now name the optional services that send data off the machine: image generation, web image search, URL import and online narration (cc8b722a, discussion #69).

unixwin/niubash ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.4Niubash v1.4.4 Stable2026-10-22T09:30:30Z

Niubash v1.4.4 packages for Windows, Linux, and macOS.

Engine: rubash git fa64d58dfb9c — every platform build in this release compiled that same revision, pinned by scripts/release/rubash-pin.json and fail-hard verified at fetch time (rubash#827). The release compiles the pinned engine, not "whatever rubash master was when the pipeline ran". Verify locally with niu --version, which prints the same revision.

Windows (x64, ARM64): Inno Setup installers (*-setup.exe) add user PATH and Windows Terminal profile integration; portable archives bundle niu with WinuxCmd and bash/sh shims.

Portable download guide (niubash#470): prefer *.tar.gz — it is hardlink-native. The bundled payload ships real NTFS hardlinks (the whole git.exe family is one shared binary, awk/gawk likewise, exactly what wpm install lays down), so the tarball is roughly half the zip's download size and unpacks to a much smaller on-disk tree; tar -xf <file>.tar.gz works out of the box on Windows 10 1803+ and any modern Linux/macOS, and on a filesystem without hardlink support (FAT32/exFAT) extraction degrades gracefully to independent byte-identical copies with no loss of function. *.7z is the smallest download (solid LZMA already collapses the shared payloads) but current 7-Zip releases restore the shared families as independent copies — every file is byte-identical, only disk usage grows. The classic *.zip is unchanged, stores every payload in full, and remains for tooling that only speaks zip.

Windows packages come with the must-install bundle pre-installed (niubash#189, niubash#209): niugit (native Windows git — the full plugin distro's five entries install through git, so a fresh machine without git.exe no longer fails niu setup), gawk 5.4.1 with plain awk working too, plus ripgrep (rg) and fd — all landing in the bundled WinuxCmd root as wpm JSON-package payloads, assembled at CI time with no runtime downloads, no runtime archive formats, and no symlinks. Pre-install is best-effort, not a promise: on legs where the package index has no matching artifact (currently niugit and gawk on the ARM64 builds; ripgrep and fd have ARM64 artifacts) the package ships WITHOUT that entry — run winuxcmd wpm install <name> once after installing to add it. The pre-install list and its recorded exclusions live in scripts/release/preinstall.json.

niubash-skill-v<version>.zip is the AI agent skill bundle (SKILL.md + references, WinuxCmd-skill layout): unpack it into an agent skills directory, or let the shell do it — niu skill install embeds the same content.

Linux (x86_64, aarch64; glibc): portable tarballs with the niu binary, README, and LICENSE — Unix builds bundle nothing, native system tools are used. Untar anywhere and run ./niu.

macOS (aarch64, x86_64): portable tarballs with the niu binary, README, and LICENSE. Untar anywhere and run ./niu.

Every artifact was smoke-verified on the build OS of the job that produced it, before upload: niu -c 'echo ok', printf 'a\nb\n' | niu -c 'sort | head -1', an offline NIU_PLUGIN_SPEC=<empty> niu plugin sync --bootstrap (exit 0, no output, no network), and a niu --version check against this tag and the engine revision.

Integrity (niubash#311): SHA256SUMS lists the SHA-256 of every shipped artifact, and each Windows installer has a .sha256 sidecar beside it. niu --self-update verifies the installer against the sidecar before running it and aborts rather than execute anything it cannot verify. The same digests feed the winget manifest InstallerSha256.

What's Changed

  • fix(release): emit version in publish-crates resolve step so the crates guard can compare by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/494
  • docs(readme): overhaul — evidence-pinned Performance section, Android baseline, gate receipts by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/493
  • docs(pages): refresh site for v1.4.3 — 24-agent roster, generated loader, pinned perf numbers by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/496
  • docs(skill): refresh agent skill bundle for the 1.4.3 surface by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/498
  • feat(msix): Microsoft Store readiness — writable LocalCache root, link family, Store self-update, WT fragments by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/497
  • fix(console): probe winuxcmd on PATH without spawning where.exe; pin test to v1.1.19 by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/506
  • docs(rc): rewrite .niubashrc.example to the manifest+loader model by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/500
  • docs(site): winget install, Android shipped, encoded-command handoff by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/502
  • docs: winget quickstart + Android platform status matches reality by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/501

OpenWhispr/openwhispr ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.10.41.10.4 Stable2026-10-22T08:21:23Z

OpenWhispr 1.10.4

Tone & Style writes your dictation, and the assistant's drafts, in the tone you pick for each kind of app. The AI Summary is more detailed by default, notes can link to a calendar event, and you can set a reasoning effort per model scope.

🌟 Highlights

  • 🎨 Tone & Style — Pick a tone for personal messages, work chats, email and everything else, and cleanup writes in it for the app you're in. Off by default; turn it on in Settings → Tone & Style.
  • 📝 Detailed AI Summary — Generate AI Summary now writes the same notes as the Detailed Notes template.
  • 📅 Calendar events on notes — Link any note to its meeting to pull in the guests and join link.
  • 🧠 Reasoning effort — Choose how hard reasoning models think, per scope.
  • ❓ Ask about OpenWhispr — The chat answers questions about the app from the docs and your settings.

✨ Also new

"This is me" in the speaker picker, searchable overflow for long sidebar folders, onboarding key checks as you type, Claude 5.5 models, and Qwen3.8 27B on Groq.

🛠 Fixes

  • Dictation: Local and self-hosted cleanup models always run when you dictate through OpenWhispr Cloud or a streaming provider, and a failed selection edit keeps your transcript.
  • Meetings and notes: Resumed recordings keep speakers apart, stopping a local meeting keeps its last words, and in-person speakers are no longer all attributed to you.
  • Transcription and AI: OpenAI live dictation uses your language and dictionary, stalled replies no longer hang, and self-hosted models stay selected after a restart.
  • Linux: Voice assistant answers paste at the caret, and paste works on niri, sway, Hyprland and KDE with non-Latin layouts.
  • Windows: Local Whisper works for user profiles with accented names.

⚠️ Heads-up

On niri, sway and Hyprland, auto-paste into Wayland apps now needs wl-clipboard.

Full details in the changelog.

Thanks to @Chadpiha, @boseq, @greatcoat, @hsusul, @IdrisGit, @clarkmaan, @Nathan-Roll1, @Adityakk9031, @etzelm, @widechaos, @Jrjy3, @jay-tau, @agn-7, @matthias-vera, @Abhishek-B-R, @atty57, @ivaschru, @isacgalvao, @tishmen, @Li-john1021 and @Sevi-py, and to @PalaceChan and @mhasgari for their bug reports. 🎉

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-10-22T05:29:08Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-1943+gd714648b8e
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1792527110

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

different-ai/openwork ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.18.60OpenWork v0.18.60 Stable2026-10-22T05:26:05Z

Move older OpenWork installs to OpenCode v2 in the background

  • Older installs move to OpenCode v2 in the background. Existing installs still using OpenCode v1 are moved over automatically, with a backup made first so your chats are kept. Windows installers also carry less duplicate engine data, and Windows can identify OpenWork's signed app files.
  • Computer use is more capable and predictable. For Mac users, the agent can script apps directly and keeps working while you use your computer; Take over and Stop return control, and Continue resumes the task. Workbot can also script supported desktop apps. Browser pages keep a steady desktop, tablet, or phone size while the panel changes, and forms take fewer steps. When available, you can dictate into OpenWork or Workbot.
  • Chat can work with more of your existing tasks and connections. Type # to add another task as context, ask the agent to create or move sidebar groups, and find connected actions with everyday wording. You can finish or repair an API-key connection in chat without sharing the key with the agent, and OpenWork Cloud tools work again in older desktop versions.
  • Documents and first launches are more dependable. Closing an edited document now gives clear save, discard, or cancel choices, and reloading or reopening it preserves your editing session and unsaved work. Workbot can open a requested document beside chat, explain why a file could not open, and show or rebuild a computer that will not connect. Fresh desktop installs can load built-in tools even if the package registry is unavailable; slow Windows sign-in and first launch no longer hide recovery options.
  • For admins: team and organization controls are clearer. Platform administrators can manage feature access per organization member and, when enabled, grant organizations Web access or extra Free Auto usage. Admins can finish connector setup after a check fails, remove connectors, and save team-role changes reliably; Workbot settings show one default model and its availability. When available, Workbot can guide eligible people from free Auto to paid Luna without losing the conversation.
  • Website visitors can find practical AI governance guidance. The expanded enterprise pages explain governing employee AI and planning an OpenWork rollout.

Full changelog: https://openworklabs.com/docs/changelog · Compare

Windows installers are signed using Microsoft Artifact Signing.

Known open fixes

Shipped without (decided by Ben, 2026-10-22: none is a regression introduced since v0.18.59; all are older fork PRs still in review): #6043, #5915, #5749, #5613, #4680, #4215, #4061, #4009, #4007, #4005, #3970, #3903, #3781, #3595, #3414, #3413. No PR opened since v0.18.59 matched the release fix filter.

esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable

View release →

studio-v2.48.0Reasonix Studio v2.48.0 Stable2026-10-22T02:29:02Z

本版大幅降低长会话和大型工作区的页面内存占用，预设模块选择支持多选，并修好记忆多进程写入互相覆盖等问题。

新增

  • 预设模块选择支持多选、搜索、全选与内置／自定义分组，手动输入 ID 移到高级选项。关联 #13988。

变更

  • 旧会话先显示列表，摘要与轮数在后台补全；读取未完成或失败会明确提示，后台索引会为打开会话让出资源。关联 #14086 by @SivanCola。

修复

  • 长会话里已完成且收起的工具调用卡片不再挂载整段输出，页面节点数和内存占用大幅下降。关联 #13899。
  • 工作区有几万个未提交文件时，“改动”列表只渲染一页，其余显示“还有 N 项没有显示”，可按 500 项展开。关联 #13899。
  • 两个进程共用一个记忆目录时，写入不再互相覆盖，也不再出现两条生效事实、改名没存下来、回滚倒退版本或索引丢行。关联 #14100 by @chuiwenwei。
  • 回滚到旧版本的记忆时，不再返回该版本从未有过的内容或报“找不到该版本”。关联 #14096 by @chuiwenwei。
  • 会话历史搜索不再把宿主追加的记忆召回和执行策略块当作用户说过的话。关联 #14076 by @chuiwenwei。
  • 读取 JSONL 检查点时不再跟随指向所指定会话目录之外的文件符号链接。关联 #14086 by @SivanCola。
  • 恢复把右侧工作区文件树里的文件和文件夹拖入对话，作为文件引用添加。关联 #12771 fixed in #12877。
  • 普通会话启动期间读取 MCP 资源时，会等待已授权的服务器连接就绪，不再因连接未完成而提前报错。关联 #14084 by @SivanCola。
  • 已登录账号时，反馈贡献榜不再错误地提示先去设置里登录。关联 #14012 fixed in #14071 by @lancy69。
  • reasonix config compact-ratio 33.3 现在写入 0.333，不再写成 0.33299999999999996。关联 #14120。
  • reasonix session 和 reasonix task 的 --dir 指向不存在的路径或文件时，报参数错误并退出码 2，不再返回空结果。关联 #14119。
  • 窗口比项目右键菜单矮时，菜单底部的操作不再被切掉，菜单限制在窗口内并可滚动。关联 #14128、#14129。

升级须知

  • 升级后首次打开会重建一次历史索引，首次打开会慢一些。关联 #14076 by @chuiwenwei。
  • 另一个进程正持有记忆目录的锁时，写入可能等约 2 秒后返回可重试的“被另一个进程锁定”，而不是悄悄写坏。关联 #14100 by @chuiwenwei。

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@SivanCola、@chuiwenwei、@lancy69

<!-- lang:en -->

This release cuts page memory in long sessions and large workspaces, adds multi-select for preset modules, and fixes memory writes overwriting each other across processes.

New

  • Preset module selection supports multi-select, search, select all and built-in/custom groups; manual ID entry moves to advanced. Refs #13988.

Changed

  • Legacy chats appear before summaries finish, which fill in the background; pending or failed reads are shown, and background indexing yields to opening a chat. Refs #14086 by @SivanCola.

Fixed

  • A finished, closed tool-call card no longer mounts its whole output, which cuts DOM nodes and memory in long sessions. Refs #13899.
  • With tens of thousands of changed files the Changes list draws one page and says how many it left out, opening 500 at a time. Refs #13899.
  • With two processes sharing one memory directory, writes no longer overwrite each other, duplicate an active subject, lose a rename, roll a revision back or drop an index line. Refs #14100 by @chuiwenwei.
  • Restoring an old memory revision no longer returns a body that revision never had or reports it missing. Refs #14096 by @chuiwenwei.
  • History search no longer treats host-appended memory-recall and execution-policy blocks as words the user said. Refs #14076 by @chuiwenwei.
  • JSONL checkpoint reads no longer follow file symlinks that point outside the supplied session directory. Refs #14086 by @SivanCola.
  • Dragging files and folders from the workbench file tree into chat as file references works again. Refs #12771 fixed in #12877.
  • Ordinary sessions wait for the authorized MCP server connection when reading resources at startup instead of failing early. Refs #14084 by @SivanCola.
  • The feedback contribution board no longer asks signed-in users to log in through Settings. Refs #14012 fixed in #14071 by @lancy69.
  • reasonix config compact-ratio 33.3 saves 0.333 instead of 0.33299999999999996. Refs #14120.
  • reasonix session and reasonix task fail with an argument error (exit 2) for a --dir that does not exist or is a file, instead of an empty result. Refs #14119.
  • When the window is shorter than a project's right-click menu, the actions at the bottom are no longer cut off; the menu stays inside the window and scrolls. Refs #14128, #14129.

Upgrade notes

  • The history index rebuilds once on first open after upgrading, so the first open is slower. Refs #14076 by @chuiwenwei.
  • While another process holds the memory directory lock, a write may wait about 2 seconds and then return a retryable "locked by another process" error instead of silently corrupting it. Refs #14100 by @chuiwenwei.

Contributors

Thanks to this release's contributors: @SivanCola, @chuiwenwei, @lancy69

  • fix(tools): normalize redundant argument envelopes / 兼容多余工具参数包装 by @SivanCola in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/14013
  • fix(guardian): roll the transcript cursor back with the review by @imcreazy in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/14046
  • fix(studio): prevent settings search result overlap by @lancy69 in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/14062
  • fix(tui): offer /quit in the slash completion menu by @esengine in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/14060
  • fix(mcp): keep add argument errors readable by @pei711 in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/14068
  • fix(mcp): import cc-switch servers without sqlite3 by @pei711 in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/14069
  • fix(serve): expose account state to the feedback board by @lancy69 in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/14071

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

astral-sh/uv ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
0.13.5 Stable2026-10-22T01:35:54Z

Release Notes

Released on 2026-10-21.

Following uv 0.13.4, which removed the affected eMudhra/emSign root from uv's own certificate store, this release updates the latest managed CPython builds for 3.11 and newer to python-build-standalone 20261021, including the CPython 3.15.1, 3.14.9, and 3.13.17 security patch releases. These builds include pip 26.2.2 both preinstalled in the managed interpreter and bundled with ensurepip, which installs pip by default when you run python -m venv. Both copies use certifi 2026.10.19, which removes the same affected root. After upgrading uv, run uv python upgrade to install the updated builds. Virtual environments pinned to an older Python patch version do not switch to the updated interpreter automatically. Upgrade pip separately in existing virtual environments.

Please note upstream CPython 3.9 and 3.10 still bundle pip releases whose certificate bundles trust the affected eMudhra/emSign root. We recommend migrating off of those versions and will no longer be releasing Docker images with uv installed for EOL Python versions.

Enhancements

  • Report incompatible self-dependencies directly in resolution errors (#21845)

Python

  • Add CPython 3.15.1, 3.14.9, and 3.13.17 (#22802)

Preview features

  • Select and rank wheel variants with no required properties or properties from PEP 850 static providers in platform-specific resolutions with wheel-variants (#22535, #22536, #22687, #22688)

Performance

  • Reduce peak memory use when resolving dependencies across multiple environments (#22769, #22770, #22784)
  • Speed up exports with complex transitive extras by resolving activation across the dependency graph (#22751)
  • Reduce uv's binary size by keeping additional error formatters out of line (#22775)
  • Reduce overhead when parsing custom build cache keys (#22749)

Bug fixes

  • Lock packages distributed only as Android wheels without a source distribution (#22806)
  • Export optional dependencies correctly when transitive extras are activated through different or incompatible environment paths (#22751)
  • Reject invalid custom build cache keys instead of silently falling back to the defaults (#22749)

Security

  • Reject certificate chains containing compromised eMudhra intermediate keys via rustls-webpki 0.103.16 (#22777)
  • Stop publishing uv Docker images based on Python 3.9 and 3.10 images that no longer receive upstream updates (#22773)

Install uv 0.13.5

Install prebuilt binaries via shell script

curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -LsSf https://releases.astral.sh/github/uv/releases/download/0.13.5/uv-installer.sh | sh

Install prebuilt binaries via powershell script

powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -c "irm https://releases.astral.sh/github/uv/releases/download/0.13.5/uv-installer.ps1 | iex"

Verifying GitHub Artifact Attestations

The artifacts in this release have attestations generated with GitHub Artifact Attestations. These can be verified by using the GitHub CLI:

gh attestation verify <file-path of downloaded artifact> --repo astral-sh/uv

You can also download the attestation from GitHub and verify against that directly:

gh attestation verify <file-path of downloaded artifact> --bundle <file-path of downloaded attestation>

earendil-works/pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.0 Stable2026-10-21T22:49:48Z

New Features

  • Image input for classifiers: codemode models.classify() accepts images for TypeSafe's Jev and Cloudflare's Clef models on typesafe, cloudflare-workers-ai, and OpenRouter. See Use classifier models and Codemode.
  • Models Configuration reference: every provider, model, model override, and compatibility field of models.json, generated from its schema. See Models Configuration.
  • Summaries without a fixed output cap: compaction and branch summaries use the model's output limit, so long reasoning no longer makes them fail. See Compaction.
  • Codemode waits for a free worker: parallel scripts and in-process subagents queue instead of failing, and PI_CODEMODE_MAX_WORKERS raises the limit. See Codemode limits.
  • Rex terminal support: OSC 8 hyperlinks, truecolor, and inline images.

Added

  • Added a Models Configuration reference generated from the models.json schema, listing all provider, model, model override, and compatibility fields (#10790)
  • Codemode models.classify() now accepts images for TypeSafe's Jev and Cloudflare's Clef models on typesafe, cloudflare-workers-ai, and OpenRouter, and lists Clef Omni and Jev 1.14 on Workers AI.
  • Provider-level compat in models.json now also applies to classifier models, with the System One fields imageInput and sendImageOptInHeader for proxies that serve another service's classifiers. Extension classifier models accept compat too.
  • Added MiniMax-M3.1-Flash to the minimax and minimax-cn providers, with thinking levels low through max (#11121)
  • Added support for the Rex terminal: OSC 8 hyperlinks, truecolor, and inline images (#11131, #11150 by @petrroll, #11183 by @nihaals)

Changed

  • Provider attribution headers now come from pi-ai's client identity. Extensions' before_provider_headers handlers no longer see the OpenRouter, NVIDIA NIM, and OpenCode attribution headers; returning null for one still removes it. Cloudflare requests send pi's detailed User-Agent instead of pi-coding-agent, as do GitHub release checks for tool downloads, and OpenCode requests send x-opencode-client whenever attribution is enabled, not only with a session.
  • generateSummary() and generateSummaryWithUsage() now ignore their reserveTokens parameter. Summary requests use the model's output limit instead.
  • Changed the default GitHub Copilot model from gpt-5.4, which Copilot retired, to gpt-6.1-sol.
  • Changed the default NVIDIA model to nvidia/nemotron-3.5-super-vl-120b-a12b (#10910 by @cv)
  • Changed the default Xiaomi model to mimo-v2.6-pro for the xiaomi and Xiaomi Token Plan providers, since mimo-v2.5-pro left the catalog.
  • Grok 4.3 and Grok 4.5 on the xai provider now offer the xhigh thinking level.
  • Sign in with ChatGPT: ChatGPT Business workspace usage-limit errors are not retried and tell the user to ask their workspace admin to increase the limit.

Fixed

  • Fixed compaction and branch summaries failing with "generation hit the token cap" when reasoning used most of the fixed summary output limit. Summaries now use the model's output limit, and compaction.reserveTokens only controls when compaction starts (#9512, #9075).
  • Fixed models.schema.json missing descriptions for provider and model fields, so editors showed no hints for them (#10790)
  • Fixed pi update failing with "Managed install marker is missing or invalid" when managed-install.json starts with a UTF-8 BOM, which Windows PowerShell 5.1 adds when it rewrites the file.
  • Fixed HTML export showing literal asterisks for Markdown bold and italic next to CJK punctuation (#3353, #10154)
  • Fixed the terminal staying in mouse-tracking, focus-reporting, Kitty keyboard, and alternate-screen modes when pi exits without its normal terminal teardown, which flooded the shell prompt with escape sequences (#11170)
  • Fixed concurrent extension select/confirm/input/editor dialogs and inline custom() components in interactive mode: a second dialog replaced the first and the first promise never settled; closing the visible dialog now shows the previous one again (#11166, #9909)
  • Fixed pasting in the shell hanging after copying text in pi and suspending it with Ctrl+Z on Linux: the background wl-copy/xclip/xsel process that keeps serving the clipboard was suspended along with pi (#11179)
  • Fixed codemode scripts failing immediately when both codemode workers are busy, for example with parallel codemode calls or in-process subagents. Scripts now wait up to 60 seconds for a free worker, and PI_CODEMODE_MAX_WORKERS raises the limit of two workers (#11184)
  • Fixed extensions failing to load when importing @earendil-works/pi-ai/models (e.g. for clampThinkingLevel) by exposing the subpath through the extension loader aliases and virtual modules (#11156)
  • Fixed mcp.json files that start with a UTF-8 BOM failing to parse, which skipped all their MCP servers and made pi mcp add/pi mcp remove fail (#11191)
  • Fixed /compact <instructions> ignoring the instructions when summarizing the start of a turn that compaction splits. When the split turn was the only new content since the last compaction, the instructions were ignored entirely (#11058)
  • Fixed resumed sessions and /tree navigation leaving extension tools inactive when the branch recorded its tools before they were registered, such as tools from extensions installed after the session started. Extension tools the branch never added or removed are now active unless they are registered with defaultActive: false (#11062, #11075)
  • Fixed sessions breaking after recovery from a response cut off at the output limit: the truncated tool call was omitted but its tool results were kept, so providers rejected every later request (#11227, #11233 by @petrroll)
  • Fixed the grep tool following RIPGREP_CONFIG_PATH, where options such as --fixed-strings silently changed regex searches into literal ones. The tool now runs rg --no-config (#11203, #11230 by @petrroll)
  • Fixed Windows shell detection rejecting runnable Microsoft Store shell aliases (#9504 by @petrroll)
  • Fixed Windows shell detection missing PowerShell outside PATH and the legacy WSL bash.exe launcher when Windows is not installed in C:\Windows. pi now checks %ProgramFiles%\PowerShell\7, %SystemRoot%\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0, and %SystemRoot% (#9501 by @petrroll)
  • Fixed GitHub Copilot sessions with large request bodies, such as many images, failing with HTTP 413 failed to parse request instead of compacting and retrying (#11016 by @petrroll)
  • Fixed the openai-codex provider offering gpt-5.5 and gpt-5.3-codex-spark, which always fail for ChatGPT accounts (#11173 by @lucasmeijer)
  • Fixed whitespace-only tool output (for example a bare \r\n from bash) making OpenAI Completions, OpenAI Responses, and Google requests fail with a 400 (#8720)
  • Fixed automatic retry not triggering for more gateway upstream failures, stream disconnected before completion errors relayed by Codex CLI proxies, and transient Google Vertex OAuth token refresh failures, and retrying errors because of digits inside request ids or byte counts (#10920, #11057, #10996)
  • Fixed OpenAI Responses models losing the prompt cache on every deferred tool load after a tool was removed (#10940)
  • Fixed Cloudflare Workers AI classifier pricing: Clef Flash costs $0.038 and Jev $0.042 per million input tokens instead of Jev being listed as free.
  • Fixed an unclosed $$ display-math block swallowing or corrupting every Markdown table after it (#11159)
  • Fixed Markdown bold and italic not rendering next to CJK punctuation (#10730 by @theBucky)
  • Fixed @ file autocomplete returning no results for queries containing regex metacharacters such as [ or ( (#10972)

obra/superpowers ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v7.0.1 Stable2026-10-21T21:05:11Z

This release retunes brainstorming after its rebuild in v7.0.0. Many of you told us, rightly, that the new default questions got annoying fast and felt formulaic. Brainstorming now digs for context before it asks anything, and tries to ask better questions. We expect to keep tuning it, but we think it's better than it was.

It also trims three long skills so they better survive compaction on Claude Code, and changes the issue and PR templates to ask for a summary written by a human.

Brainstorming

  • It digs for context first. In an existing project, or when you point it at a spec, plan, or files, it reads them before its first message and doesn't ask what they already answer. (#2560)
  • It tries to ask better questions. v7.0.0 opened every session the same way: an offer to skip the questions, then a generic question about "the moment you wished this existed." Now, when you've already said what you want, it plays your request back with what it found in the project and asks about the one thing still open. Later questions build on your answers and on what it found, and say what the answer would change. (#2560, #2571)
  • It offers to skip the questions only when it started on its own. If you asked for brainstorming by name or with /brainstorming, it no longer offers to skip it. (#2560)
  • It lays out the approaches and writes a design doc before building. It presents two or three ways to build the thing, recommends one, and asks you to pick. Then it writes a design doc, a short one for a small change, and builds nothing until you approve it. Some of you saw it skip the doc and start building; in one of our test scenarios, it skipped the doc without asking in 24 of 24 runs before this change and 3 of 24 after. (#2571)

Thanks to @mbcrawfo for opening #2530, and to @katyatitkova, @ertra, @blorenz, @Biomaj, @fsalum, @technicalpickles, @orochi235, @alexxburg, @fcavalieri, @CelesteCunha, and @zzzzz9999 for the reports, transcripts, and testing there.

If brainstorming's questions in 7.0.1 still don't feel right, please comment on #2530. Examples help most: what you asked, what it asked back, and what you wish it had asked.

Skills better survive compaction on Claude Code

After a compaction, Claude Code re-attaches each skill cut off at 20,000 characters, so anything past that point is gone for the rest of the session. In one reported session, subagent-driven development lost its five-round limit and ran 18 fix rounds on one task. Thanks @dan-1492 for the report. (#2481)

  • subagent-driven-development moves the fix loop, final review, report handling, resuming, preflight, batching, and cannot-verify guidance into references/. It reads each file from disk at the step that needs it. (#2565)
  • writing-skills moves its supporting docs, the SKILL.md template and discovery guidance, bulletproofing, and flowchart conventions into references/. (#2564)
  • executing-plans drops its Example Workflow, which repeated rules stated above it. (#2563)
  • tests/skill-size/test-skill-size.sh fails when any SKILL.md reaches 20,000 bytes. (#2565)

Contributing

  • Issue and PR templates now ask who is filing and what their human partner asked for, plus a summary of under 200 words written by a human. The bug report also asks how you confirmed the problem doesn't happen without Superpowers. (#2569)
  • We don't accept agent-written PRs that fix someone else's issue unless a maintainer asked for one. If your human partner hit the same problem, add their report to the existing issue. The PR template, AGENTS.md, and the README say so. (#2569)
  • diagnosing-superpowers adds the same two sections to the issues it drafts and won't file one while the summary is a placeholder. Its prefilled link now opens the Bug Report template; we removed the Session Diagnosis Report template. (#2569)

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.12.6 Stable2026-10-21T21:02:53Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added the prompt-cache-persistent catalog axis (isPromptCachePersistent) for providers whose own endpoint never expires prompt caches; logged-in OpenAI Codex declares it

Changed

  • Updated the bundled Yolo-Auto catalog to its current models, qwen3.8-27b (now the default), yolo, yolo-small, and yolo-128k, and removed the retired deepseek-flash-v4 and qwen3.8-flash

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • Added the _session/steering ACP extension (advertised as _meta.steering.supported), so clients such as agent-shell can steer a running turn; a steer sent while a permission dialog is open cancels that dialog, and _meta.steering.queue: "followUp" queues a follow-up instead (#15885 by @marcintustin)
  • /mcp reauth <name> now works over ACP, RPC, and print mode (omp -p '/mcp reauth <name>'): it opens the browser, prints the authorization URL, reconnects the server in the running session, and print mode exits 1 when reauthorization fails (#16046 by @marcintustin)

Changed

  • ACP session/cancel now also cancels prompts that are queued or still dispatching, and stops autonomous runs, instead of being ignored when no client turn is streaming (#15885 by @marcintustin)
  • Idle compaction no longer runs on logged-in OpenAI Codex accounts, whose prompt cache never expires; compacting while idle only threw away a warm cache
  • ACP sessions no longer fail to start when a client-declared MCP server cannot connect; the failure is reported in the session, and an HTTP/SSE server can be repaired with /mcp reauth (#16046 by @marcintustin)
  • /mcp reauth, /mcp test, and /mcp unauth now pick the project entry when a server is defined in both project and user config, and skip project config when mcp.enableProjectConfig is off (#16046 by @marcintustin)
  • /mcp reauth removes the server's stale Authorization header from its config when it writes the new OAuth binding (#16046 by @marcintustin)
  • omp -p '/compact', /handoff, and /retry given as CLI arguments now run the command instead of sending the text to the model, and exit 1 when the command fails; slash text arriving on stdin or from @file is still sent to the model (#16387 by @marcintustin)
  • Interactive startup no longer waits for extensions that register only tools, commands, flags, renderers, or event hooks (for example MCP bridge plugins): they load once the composer accepts input, and your first message waits for them instead. An extension is deferred only after one launch has confirmed it registers no providers, shortcuts, composer shapes, or file fallbacks. Launches that resume a session, pass --tools, or pass flags omp does not recognize still load every extension first (#16684 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • Fixed subagents whose role has an explicitly empty fallback chain falling back through the default chain when the default role uses the same model, including after a retry fallback, prewalk or context promotion briefly switched models or the subagent was revived (#16548 by @eggpeat)
  • Fixed /usage and omp usage --json listing identity-less OAuth accounts such as Devin as missing usage beside their working reports (#16549 by @eggpeat)
  • Fixed releasing or reaping a browser tab returning while Chromium still listed the page; the close now waits until Chromium reports the tab destroyed (#16680 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed the advisor prompt forbidding all repeated advice, including real escalations: advisors may now re-raise a prior nit or concern as a blocker once new evidence makes it one (#13887, #16484 by @pjh890321)
  • Fixed Esc no longer interrupting in the TUI after a second MCP OAuth login replaced an unfinished first one (#16046 by @marcintustin)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Changed

  • In Tern, centered sheets (/context, reports, keyboard shortcuts, BTW history, session info, the pause screen) fill the pane when it is narrower than 100 columns instead of floating with margins (#16676 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • Fixed the /agents and /models hubs in Tern squeezing the left sidebar as long lines (such as a streaming new-agent draft) widen the body pane

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Added

  • Added RpcConnection.requestCancellable() and Client.requestPermission(params, { signal }); client connections abort a handler and answer -32800 when the peer sends $/cancel_request, while agent connections keep running the request and return its real result (#15885 by @marcintustin)

What's Changed

  • fix(coding-agent): clarify blocker escalation in advisor prompt by @pjh890321 in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16484
  • feat(coding-agent): support ACP _session/steering for mid-turn client steering by @marcintustin in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15885
  • feat(acp): support /mcp reauth outside the TUI by @marcintustin in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16046
  • feat(tui): fill narrow Tern panes with centered sheets and pickers by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16676
  • fix(coding-agent): confirm browser tab close and repair red CI tests by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16680
  • perf(coding-agent): load startup-safe extensions after the composer accepts input by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16684
  • fix(coding-agent): keep explicitly empty subagent fallback chains empty by @eggpeat in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16548
  • fix(coding-agent): stop listing identity-less OAuth accounts as missing usage by @eggpeat in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16549

zed-industries/zed ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
v1.26.1-pre Prerelease2026-10-21T20:55:47Z

This week's release includes Pyrefly as an optional built-in Python language server, the ability to expand and inspect nested variable values directly in debugger hovers, and DWARF debugging for WebAssembly sources in the JavaScript debugger.

Features

AI

  • Improved fuzzy searching in agent model and configuration option pickers for Zed Agent and third-party ACP agents, while keeping search preparation off the UI thread. (#64147; thanks JacobNWolf)
  • Improved OpenCode compatibility when replaying DeepSeek reasoning and disabling thinking for Google models. (#65922)
  • Improved thinking controls to explain when thinking cannot be disabled at the selected effort level. (#62480)

Debugger

  • Added DWARF debugging to the JavaScript debugger to support debugging WebAssembly sources. (#65371; thanks SGumbles)
  • Improved debugger hovers with an expandable, keyboard-accessible variable tree. (#53198; thanks Tortes)

Languages

  • Added Pyrefly as an optional built-in Python language server, available when enabled in the Python language_servers setting. (#44262; thanks grievejia)
  • Improved restarting language servers after stopping them with the LSP button. (#65387)
  • Improved SQL syntax highlighting in Rust sql! string literals. (#58778; thanks kuskhan)

Remote Development

  • Improved SSH reconnections by starting the resync timeout only after the handshake completes. (#65457; thanks Huliiiiii)

macOS

  • Added support for the Display P3 color space on macOS with the use_display_p3 setting. (#64394; thanks MDalstrom)

Other

  • Added an extension option to project_panel.sort_order to group files by extension. (#65776; thanks Carnage725)
  • Improved fuzzy search ranking in the File Finder. (#59224; thanks feitreim)

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed discarding unstaged changes in the Git Panel also discarding staged changes. (#65559; thanks stezu)
  • Fixed hangs when restoring agent threads with subagents. (#65177)
  • Fixed a crash in the Agent Panel when mentioning a file whose name contains a line break, and improved how those mentions display line breaks. (#65693)
  • Fixed a bug where cancelling the save-changes prompt while removing a project from the sidebar still removed it from the recent projects list. (#65683; thanks mateioprea)
  • Fixed agent authentication overwriting drafts. (#65844)
  • Fixed agent configuration defaults being saved before acceptance or ignoring the agent's returned value. (#65849)
  • Fixed duplicated thinking text from OpenAI-compatible providers that send both reasoning and reasoning_content. (#65926)
  • Fixed file icons not appearing for stem-based filename variants. (#51891; thanks bryanpth)
  • Fixed Google AI invalid API keys being classified as invalid requests instead of authentication errors. (#65885)
  • Fixed IME composition text not being underlined in single-line inputs and editors with diagnostics turned off. (#65610; thanks Sha1rholder)
  • Fixed JSON completion ordering for enum values in settings.json. (#65589; thanks ragen1337)
  • Fixed Markdown preview tabs for files with the same name not showing their directory. (#65736; thanks gitKashish)
  • Fixed Mistral Large 4 responses failing when used through an OpenAI-compatible provider. (#65915)
  • Fixed OpenCode requests failing for Claude models that use adaptive thinking block binding. (#65925)

v1.25.2 Stable2026-10-21T20:54:49Z

This week's release includes smaller Zed binaries across macOS, Windows, and Linux, the ability to apply inlay hint text edits by double-clicking, context compaction and session notices for external agents, and new GPT-6 Astra, Claude Fable 5.1, and Claude Opus 5.5 models on Amazon Bedrock.

AI

  • Added GPT-6 Astra, Claude Fable 5.1, and Claude Opus 5.5 to the Amazon Bedrock provider. (#64201, #64685; thanks IbrahimKhan12 and alexandergunnarson)

Git

  • Improved startup time when restoring hundreds of editor selections. (#65171)
