srbhr/Resume-Matcher ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.01.4 Short Circuit Stable2026-10-21T20:38:33Z

Resume Matcher 1.4: Short Circuit

Short Circuit makes the resume itself better, not only better matched. Every tailoring run now critiques its own writing and rewrites the weak lines without adding a single fact, you can push a missing job keyword in when you confirm you have it, and you can watch each stage of the work as it happens. It also brings agent mode, an MCP server, new templates and multi-page resumes, and a round of security hardening.

Named after Short Circuit from Daft Punk's Discovery, this release continues the v1 series after 1.3 Crescendolls.

What's new

Writing that holds up

  • Critique and rewrite in every tailoring run. Fixed rules and an AI critic check each bullet and the summary. Lines with an em dash, a weak opener such as "Responsible for", or no stated outcome get one rewrite. A fact check drops any rewrite that adds or loses a number, name, tool or skill, so your work stays on the page and nothing is invented. Light nudge only critiques.
  • Writing score. A 0 to 100 score from the fixed rules (the same with every AI provider), with before and after, the lines to fix and the reviewer's notes.
  • Improve writing in the Builder. A new Writing tab scores any resume, master included, and rewrites its weak lines. You review every change before it goes into the editor.
  • Heavy rewrite. A fourth tailoring mode for bigger edits: full Challenge, Action, Result rewrites, a new summary and reordered bullets, with a clear hallucination warning.
  • Evidence-based cover letters. One page, usually 200 to 300 words: a hook tied to the job, two or three matching examples, a company-fit paragraph grounded in the job description and a specific close. Contributed by @AlexanderMorris2k.

Keywords on your terms

  • Add missing keywords. In the review dialog, missing job keywords become chips. Keywords your master shows are applied directly; a keyword it does not show needs your "I have this". Each one goes into your skills plus at most two lines where it fits, and nothing else is added around it.
  • See where they went. The dialog lists where every keyword landed, marks it in the changed lines and shows the ATS score before and after. JD Match highlights the keywords you added in blue, and interview prep asks about them first.
  • One match number. JD Match now shows the same keyword match as the ATS score, and keeps C++, .NET, Node.js and CI/CD whole.
  • A sharper ATS score. It now scores the job title and date consistency, and the builder keeps the last calculated score with a Recalculate button. Contributed by @himalyansailor.
  • ATS parse check. A new ATS CHECK tab shows whether a plain text extractor can read your exported PDF in the right order.

Watch it work

  • Live progress. Tailoring shows each real stage as it runs, with a note when a slow model needs a minute.
  • Tailoring queue. Queue several job descriptions and tailor them one after another, with cooldowns that respect rate limits. Contributed by @coolb0y.

Use it your way

  • Agent mode. Open Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Copilot or Antigravity in the repo and ask it to tailor your resume: no API key, and the web app updates live as the agent saves.
  • MCP server. Its tools let any MCP client drive Resume Matcher against the same database as the app. Contributed by @rrvrs.
  • Claude Code and ChatGPT plans as providers. Run on a Claude Code install with no API key (contributed by @GongYuanCaiJi), pick its model, or sign in with a ChatGPT plan, with a clear note on subscription risk.
  • Optional PostgreSQL for larger or shared setups.

More control over the resume

  • Several master resumes. Keep up to five, one per career track; tailoring picks the strongest bullets with code, not the AI.
  • Page limit and bullet cap settings for tailoring. Contributed by @himalyansailor.
  • New templates: Deedy, Modern Deedy and Vivid Single Column, plus multi-page resumes with predictable page breaks and an academic CV mode.
  • The PDF matches the preview, and each resume keeps its own formatting.
  • Reorder everything: projects, custom section items (contributed by @Bdysj) and the bullets within an entry.

Security

  • Saved API keys can no longer be sent to arbitrary servers (#1045, reported and fixed by @MahediBuilds). An unauthenticated caller could point a stored key at their own server; a saved custom Base URL is now bound to the key that confirmed it.
  • Local by default (#1048). The app has no login, so it now listens on loopback only and rejects unexpected Host headers.
  • LiteLLM. Resume Matcher pins litellm==1.104.2 and was not affected by the compromised LiteLLM 1.107.0 release of 2026-10-21. Keep that pin in mind before upgrading LiteLLM.
  • Dependency updates: cryptography 50.0.0, Next.js 16.3.3, DOMPurify 3.4.13, undici 7.30.0, sharp 0.35.4, js-yaml 4.3.2 and Vitest.

Upgrading from 1.3

Back up the complete data directory first, as always: the database, config.json, uploaded files and the hidden .secret_key together.

  • The app now listens on 127.0.0.1 only. If you open it from another machine or a reverse proxy, set BIND_ADDRESS (Docker Compose) or HOST (source installs) and add the host name to ALLOWED_HOSTS, for example BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0 ALLOWED_HOSTS=192.168.1.20 docker compose up -d. See SETUP.md for details.
  • Database changes are additive and run on startup: new columns for ATS scores, per-resume formatting and forced keywords.
  • Source installs still need Node.js 22+ and Python 3.13+; reinstall dependencies after pulling.

Docker images

Release tag: v1.4.0. Versioned images for Linux amd64 and arm64 publish to GHCR and Docker Hub:

docker pull ghcr.io/srbhr/resume-matcher:1.4.0
# or
docker pull srbhr/resume-matcher:1.4.0

The image tags are 1.4.0 and 1.4.

Verification

The release passed 1,944 backend tests and 1,596 frontend tests with type checking. The writing loop, live progress and keyword forcing were also run end to end against a real model through the app's proxy.

Thank you

Thank you to everyone who contributed code, reported bugs on Discord and GitHub, tested fixes and reviewed this release.

Contributors to this release: @576657922, @AlexanderMorris2k, @Bdysj, @coolb0y, @Frankli9986, @gingeekrishna, @GongYuanCaiJi, @himalyansailor, @HT-Moh, @kobihikri, @MahediBuilds, @rrvrs, @srbhr.

Automation contributions are credited to @dependabot.

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.8.185 Stable2026-10-21T19:44:10Z

HyperFrames v0.8.185

Released on 2026-10-21.

hyperframes cloud render --resolution 4k now works for transparent MOV and WebM. A render no longer fails when its audio peak stalls under correction, check accepts projects that inline the GSAP library, and cloud render chunks start their capture browsers while video frames extract. Capture motion blur arrives as an opt-in on the composition root.

Features

  • CLI: cloud render --resolution 4k accepts --format mov and --format webm; the CLI and the render server no longer refuse 4K with a transparent format (25126bca6, #6045).
  • Core: Capture motion blur: data-motion-blur on the composition root blurs fast-moving elements across sub-frame samples, with shutter angle and sample count settings (0f1d6908f, #5967); it follows the render's device scale, so 2x and 4K renders blur at full resolution (ffcef8f88, #5968).

Fixes

  • Producer: A render whose AAC true peak stalls above the limit during correction no longer fails at assemble (32ebdde61, #6043).
  • Lint: check no longer fails a project that pastes the GSAP library inline, and a bundle around GSAP never inherits its exemption (50e7e8df6, #6018).
  • Engine: A capture browser whose BeginFrame probe passed keeps BeginFrame capture when closing the probe page is slow (a8b576d15, #6037).

Performance

  • Producer: A cloud render chunk starts its capture browsers while its video frames are still being extracted, so heavy chunks finish sooner and cost less (0716670fa, #6031).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.184...v0.8.185

v0.8.184 Stable2026-10-21T16:18:00Z

HyperFrames v0.8.184

Released on 2026-10-21.

hyperframes cloud render now takes projects whose media is far larger than the project upload limit. Multi-worker mp4 renders start encoding while they capture, Lambda chunks run more capture browsers at once, and a heavy project's preview builds faster. Transparent webm keeps its transparency in Studio previews, and a color grade applies to an image that appears after its scene starts hidden.

Features

  • CLI: cloud render uploads media of 5 MB or more as hosted assets before zipping the project, so projects with media far larger than the upload limit render with no manual step. Uploads are cached, so a re-render sends only new or changed media (b306438e8, #5931).

Fixes

  • Studio Server: A transparent VP8 or VP9 webm, such as remove-background output, keeps its transparency in the preview copy that check, Studio and publish make (d67e81c7f, #6025).
  • Core: A color-graded image renders graded in snapshot and render when it is hidden as its scene starts and revealed later (087451837, #6012).
  • CLI: A preview refused inside a host app no longer stops other Chrome processes left behind by earlier runs (5641a9179, #6022).

Performance

  • Producer: A multi-worker mp4 render starts encoding while its frames are still being captured, instead of after, with byte-identical output (eec3c51d7, #5952).
  • AWS Lambda: Each chunk renders with as many capture browsers as the function can host, five instead of three on a 10 GB function, so heavy 4K chunks finish sooner (cde21630e, #6026).
  • Core: A heavy project's first preview page builds sooner, without rescanning its styles once per composition variable (0d51eb0de, #5893).
  • Core: A page build reads fonts, inlined assets and scenes mounted many times only once, and an offline build with a warm font cache makes no network request (884d8690a, #5954).

Internal

  • Core: The building blocks of capture motion blur (element poses, finding fast movers, the per-frame draw). Nothing uses them yet (8df2d8d95, #5875).
  • CLI: The capture network tests no longer flake on CI (cfdddd8a9, #6024).
  • A hung Windows test run keeps its log, and commit hooks lint .mjs and .cjs files (2e1cf69e0, #6023).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.183...v0.8.184

NousResearch/hermes-agent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.22.4Hermes Agent v0.22.4 Stable2026-10-21T18:49:24Z

Hermes Agent v0.22.4

Release Date: October 21, 2026

Patch release. Desktop stops showing suggestion pills above the composer, plus small plugin and dashboard fixes, for the CLI and installer, the Microsoft Store app, Docker and Hermes Cloud.

<!-- HERMES_BUILDS_TABLE -->

Fixes

  • Desktop no longer shows suggestion pills above the composer. The pills appeared for no clear reason, mostly nudging you to set up MCP servers. They and the helpers that only they used are gone. (#142559)
  • Plugins that install nothing skip the dependency consent step. A plugin that declares no dependencies, or whose pyproject.toml installs nothing, now enables without an empty consent prompt. (#142569)
  • Dashboard: WhatsApp can be safely re-paired after logging out. (#142439)
  • Desktop plugin review: the surface lint no longer flags prototype writes; reviewers check them by hand. (#142586)
  • Desktop: the approval-mode chip follows the active profile's mode. (#142565)
  • Release builds: the desktop bundle's pre-build check gets enough time to verify archived build inputs when storage is slow. (#142623)

Also new in the plugin catalog: bubblewrap (bwrap terminal backend on Linux), Singularity/Apptainer, mcp-serve (Hermes as an MCP server), agent-importers (hermes import-agent, now with Cursor), inbox and workflow-record from Hermes Labs. Telegram Business moves to the Hermes Labs community tier.

About this release

Measured at commit 4d04303818b, the window since v0.22.3 contains 14 non-merge commits across 82 changed files (+1,475 / −3,022) and 13 merged PRs.

Updating

  • Microsoft Store app: updates through the Store.
  • Desktop app installed from the installer (.exe / .dmg): no change in this release; it updates with the next bundled release.
  • CLI: hermes update, or re-run the installer one-liner.
  • Docker / Hermes Cloud: nousresearch/hermes-agent:stable (or :latest).

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.18CC Switch v4.0.18 Stable2026-10-21T16:16:23Z

CC Switch v4.0.18

本版本新增对 Antigravity CLI（agy）的支持，它是 CC Switch 管理的第 11 个应用。你可以为 agy 添加官方登录卡和第三方 API Key 卡。注意：Antigravity 的供应商只在从 CC Switch 卡片「使用此供应商打开终端」启动的 agy 里生效，在其他终端里直接运行 agy 不会用到它；MCP、Skills 和提示词同步给 agy，会话可以浏览和恢复，用量从会话日志导入；「应用」页可以安装、升级、卸载 agy。macOS 上，授权中心还能管理多个 Antigravity 账号：一键切换 agy 的登录，查看每个账号的额度，把官方卡绑定到某个账号。另外，Windows 上的应用内更新不会再因为文件被占用而重启电脑，MSI 安装时可以选择不创建桌面快捷方式；页头改为在应用名旁用小徽章显示实际的连接。还修复了 macOS 上并发请求多时路由因文件句柄耗尽而失败、从访达启动时「重启 Codex 守护进程」报 env: node、Claude Code 会话用 /cd 换目录后仍归在旧项目下、Windows 上打开终端后一直转圈直到关掉终端窗口、Codex 未使用的额度窗口显示倒计时、DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 没有计价等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

Antigravity CLI（agy）

Google 的 Antigravity CLI（命令是 agy）现在是一个独立的应用，有自己的供应商、MCP、Skills、提示词、会话和用量，和 Gemini CLI 分开管理（#8268、#2959）。

⚠️ Antigravity 的供应商必须从 CC Switch 以终端启动才生效。 和其他应用不同，CC Switch 不改 agy 自己的设置文件，所以 Antigravity 的卡片没有「切换」，也没有「使用中」。要用哪张卡，就点那张卡上的「使用此供应商打开终端」，在弹出的终端里运行的 agy 才会用这家供应商；在其他终端里直接运行 agy，用的仍是 agy 自己的 Google 登录和设置。（macOS 上在授权中心切换账号是例外：它改的是 agy 的全局登录，见下一节。）

  • 两种供应商卡。 官方登录卡用 agy 自己的 Google 登录，打开终端时用卡上选的模型启动 agy，只对这次会话生效；第三方卡填兼容 Gemini API 的地址和 Key，地址留空用 Google 默认的 API，Key 和地址只交给这个终端里的 agy，不写进 agy 的配置和 shell 启动文件（启动用的临时文件会自动删除）。模型列表来自 agy 自带的模型目录，填的是 agy 认得的显示名称。
  • MCP、Skills、提示词。 同步到 ~/.gemini/config，这是 agy 的全局定制目录，可能和同一台电脑上的 Antigravity IDE 共用。第三方卡启动的 agy 同样能用这些 MCP、Skills 和提示词。
  • 会话。 可以浏览 agy 和 Antigravity IDE 的会话，只显示你输入的内容，不显示 agy 自己附加的上下文；第三方卡启动的会话也能找到，并用同一张卡恢复。
  • 用量。 从会话日志导入（包括 Antigravity IDE 的会话），按每轮选择的模型记账，费用按价格表里对应模型的 API 价格估算。
  • 官方额度。 在官方卡上开启额度查询后，显示 Gemini 和 Claude/GPT 两组额度，每组有 5 小时和每周两个窗口；卡片上每组只显示剩得少的那个窗口，点开下拉可以看全部四个窗口和各自的重置时间。
  • 「应用」页管理 agy。 用官方安装脚本安装，用 agy update 升级（Homebrew 安装的也一样），可以卸载。
  • 深链接也可以导入 agy 的供应商、MCP 和提示词。

授权中心管理多个 Antigravity 账号（macOS）

agy 同时只能登录一个账号。现在授权中心多了 Antigravity 一组：点「用 Google 登录」或「添加账号」，在浏览器里添加账号，agy 里已经登录的账号也会自动收进来。

  • 切换。 在账号的菜单里选「切换 agy 到这个账号」，之后新开的 agy 都用这个号。原来登录的号会先收回授权中心，切走也不会丢。如果 agy 正在运行，会提醒你：已经打开的 agy 还用原来的号，而且可能把原来的号写回去，最好先退出。
  • 每个账号的额度。 每一行都显示自己的额度，不用切过去才能看。查额度只读额度，不调用模型。不能使用 Antigravity 的账号（比如有年龄限制的）会显示 Google 给出的原因，而不是一句看不懂的 403。
  • 把官方卡绑定到账号。 官方卡的表单里可以选一个账号：打开终端前会先把 agy 的登录切到这个号，已经是它就不再写入；卡片上显示的也是这个号的额度。不选就跟随 agy 当前的登录。卡片同步到另一台电脑后，那台电脑上也添加了这个号才能用，否则打开终端时会提示，不会用别的号启动。
  • 删除账号不会让 agy 退出登录。 CC Switch 只删掉自己保存的那一份。

改进

Windows 上的应用内更新不会再重启电脑（#8628、#8534）。应用内更新以不弹对话框的方式运行 MSI 安装包，以前只要安装目录里有文件还被占用，Windows Installer 就会不提示直接重启电脑。现在安装包本身禁止重启，从旧版本更新到这一版时也生效。

MSI 安装时可以不创建桌面快捷方式（#8601、#5667）。安装目录页多了「Create a desktop shortcut」选项，默认勾上；静默安装可以加 CREATEDESKTOPSHORTCUT=0 跳过。升级时保留现在的状态：你删掉的快捷方式不会被重新创建。桌面目录写不进去时也不会再让安装失败。

页头显示实际的连接（#8634）。实际连到哪里，从模式标签旁挪到应用名旁，用当前模式（直连、路由、聚合）的颜色画成一个小徽章；切到别的模式标签看时也一直显示，名称太长会截断，鼠标悬停可以看到全部。

精简界面上的说明文字（#8633）。供应商表单、OpenClaw、每日记忆、同步和整流器设置里，和旁边标签、标题重复的说明去掉了，补充说明挪进已有的帮助卡片；会影响结果的提示（费用、重启、同步、删除等）照常显示。

用量页的应用筛选只列「应用」页里显示的应用。 和会话管理一样：显示 Claude Desktop 时 Claude Code 也保留，当前选中的应用即使被隐藏也不会消失。

预设： 新增 DIT.ai、hao.ai。

修复

  • macOS 上并发请求多时路由失败（#8668）。从访达或登录项启动的应用，同时能打开的文件数只有 256 个。路由每个进行中的请求要占一个客户端连接和一个上游连接，并发一多就用完了：接不了新请求、连不上上游，连配置文件和数据库都打不开。现在启动时把这个上限调高到系统允许的值（最多 10240）。Linux 同样处理。
  • 从访达启动时，「重启 Codex 守护进程」报 env: node: No such file or directory（#8653）。从访达启动时 CC Switch 只有系统目录的 PATH，npm 安装的 codex 要靠 node 运行，node 装在别的目录时就找不到。现在运行这类命令时会先加上登录 shell 的 PATH，和安装、升级工具时一样。OpenCode 获取模型列表也受这个问题影响，一并修复。
  • Claude Code 会话里用 /cd 换了目录，会话管理里仍归在旧项目下（#8643、#6862）。复制的恢复命令也会先 cd 到旧目录，在那里 claude --resume 找不到这个会话。现在和 Claude Code 自己的规则一样，按最后一次换到的目录归类，进出 worktree 也一样。
  • Windows 上打开终端后，按钮一直转圈，直到关掉那个终端窗口（#6712）。现在终端打开后就会结束等待。
  • Codex 没用过的额度窗口也显示重置倒计时（#8631）。用量为 0 的窗口，Codex 返回的重置时间是「现在加一个窗口长度」，所以每次刷新倒计时都会跳回整个窗口（5 小时窗口跳回 5 小时）。现在用量为 0 的窗口不显示重置时间。
  • DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 的请求显示「未定价」、按 0 元计。 OpenCode Go、OpenRouter 和 Hermes 预设用的模型名是 deepseek-v4.1-flash，以前价格表里没有。现在按 DeepSeek Flash 的官方价格计算；已经记录的请求不会重新计价。
  • 手册里「官方额度用完后 Codex 锁住发送键」的绕开办法已失效，已更正（#7962）。4.0.16 起聚合模式会保留 ChatGPT 登录，把第三方设为聚合的默认供应商已经绕不开。现在的办法是：切到直连，确认「设置 → 应用配置 → Codex」里的「非接管切换时保留官方登录」是关闭的，切到一家第三方供应商后彻底重启 Codex；额度重置后再切回聚合。

升级说明

数据库版本从 20 升到 21，为 MCP 和 Skills 增加 Antigravity 的开关。升级前会自动备份数据库；升级后再退回旧版本，旧版本会提示数据库版本过新，打不开。

  • Antigravity 的供应商只在从卡片「使用此供应商打开终端」启动的 agy 里生效，在其他终端里直接运行 agy 不会用到它。
  • Antigravity 默认显示在「应用」页，不用的话可以在那里隐藏。
  • 在授权中心管理 Antigravity 账号目前只支持 macOS。其他平台不显示账号选择，官方卡跟随 agy 自己的登录；从 Mac 同步过来的绑定会被忽略。

致谢

感谢 @Owlbay、@peterLwk888、@ajia1206（Antigravity CLI 支持）、@YUZHEthefool（Windows 更新不再重启、可选桌面快捷方式）、@funkpopo（可选桌面快捷方式）、@SaladDay（页头连接徽章、精简说明文字）、@Dopetaiga（/cd 后的会话归类）、@cuiyf5516（Codex 未使用的额度窗口）、@umutcankurt（Windows 打开终端一直等待）的贡献。

感谢 @lm902、@zhufeizzz、@AisinGioroKiIoi、@WzLYVg387U、@mvdorofeev、@dw2286、@LeenixP 报告问题、提供复现信息和修复思路。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

cli/cli ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.103.0GitHub CLI 2.103.0 Stable2026-10-21T15:33:31Z

Expiring, auto-refreshing OAuth tokens (preview)

gh can now sign in with expiring OAuth credentials that it refreshes automatically. When you opt in, gh stores a short-lived access token alongside a rotating refresh token and refreshes the access token whenever it is needed, so you stay signed in without having to authenticate again.

To opt in for a single sign-in, run:

gh auth login --expiring

To prefer expiring credentials for every sign-in, run:

gh config set expiring_credentials enabled

gh auth status shows whether a credential is refreshable and when the current access token expires. Both gh auth status and gh auth token automatically refresh the access token when needed. Expiring tokens are available on github.com and GHE.com; on hosts that do not issue them, gh falls back to a regular non-expiring token.

If you use HTTPS as your git protocol, we advise using git 2.46 or newer and letting gh act as your git credential helper by running gh auth setup-git. This way gh serves and refreshes the expiring credential for git, and git does not cache it past its lifetime.

[!IMPORTANT] Expiring access tokens should not be cached. Scripts and extensions that store the output of gh auth token will eventually hold an expired token, so run gh auth token again each time a token is needed. Tokens supplied through GH_TOKEN or GITHUB_TOKEN are never refreshed.

For more information, see Authenticating with GitHub CLI.

Manage issue artifacts with gh issue artifact (private preview)

gh issue artifact brings issue artifacts to the command line. Users and organizations enrolled in the private preview can use the list, view, create, edit, download, and delete commands to manage issue artifacts.

# Create a Markdown document with an attached image
gh issue artifact create 142 signin-plan.md --attach ./signin-flow.png

# Create an HTML document
gh issue artifact create 142 menu-prototype.html

# List or view artifacts
gh issue artifact list 142
gh issue artifact view 142 2

# Update a document or restore an earlier version
gh issue artifact edit 142 2 --body-file signin-plan-v2.md
gh issue artifact edit 142 2 --restore-version 5

Artifacts can also be addressed by their GitHub URLs, and Markdown documents support image and video attachments with --attach.

[!IMPORTANT] Issue artifacts are in private preview. These commands only work for repositories enrolled in the preview. See the Issue Artifacts private preview announcement and feedback discussion for access instructions and to share feedback.
